remaining_burn + safety_buffer - current - inbound)\n\n        if additional_needed > 0:\n            raw_tankers = additional_needed / tanker_cap\n            tankers_rounded = timedelta(days=math.floor(float(delivered_doh or 0)))\n            else:\n                required_refill_date = projected_runout\n\n        if (\n            tankers_rounded > 0\n            and
